function u0.through(a1, a2, a3, a4)

    -- upvalues: u0 (val), u23 (val), turn (val)

return {
    aimDirection = function(a1, a2, a3) -- Line: 13; types: a1: vector, a2: number, a3: number
        local v1 = Vector3.new(a1.X, 0, a1.Z)
        local Magnitude = v1.Magnitude

        if Magnitude < 0.001 then
            if 0.001 < (math.abs(a1.Y)) then
                return (Vector3.new(0, math.sign(a1.Y), 0))
            end

            return nil
        end

        if a3 <= 0 then
            return a1.Unit
        end

        local v2 = a2 * a2
        local v3 = v2 * v2 - a3 * (a3 * Magnitude * Magnitude + a1.Y * 2 * v2)

        if not (v3 >= 0) then
            return nil
        end

        return (v1.Unit + Vector3.new(0, (v2 - math.sqrt(v3)) / (a3 * Magnitude), 0)).Unit
    end,
}

function u0.parse(a1) -- Line: 30; upvalues: u0 (val); types: a1: string

    if v1 == "" then
        return nil
    end

    local v4 = 1

    end

    if v3 and v3 == v3 and v3 ~= (1 / 0) and v3 ~= (-1 / 0) then
        if v3 < 0 then
            return nil
        end

    end

    return nil
end

function u0.nextAction(a1, a2, a3, a4)
    -- Line: 850
    -- upvalues: u0 (val)
    -- types: a1: string, a2: table, a3: number, a4: function

    local Chicken = u0.Routines[a1] or u0.Routines.Chicken

    if a1 == "Sheep" then
        v2 = {"Idle", "Graze", "Walk"}
        a2.step = a2.step % #v2 + 1
        v1 = v2[a2.step]
    else
        local everySeconds

        if a1 == "Cow" then
            if not (a2.walkCheckAt <= a3) then
                v1 = if not (a4() < 0.5) then "Graze" else "Idle"
            else
                everySeconds = u0.CowWalk.everySeconds
                a2.walkCheckAt = a3
                    + (everySeconds[1] + a4() * (everySeconds[2] - everySeconds[1]))
                v1 = if not (a4() < u0.CowWalk.chance) then "Graze" else "Walk"
            end
        elseif a1 ~= "Horse" then
            v2 = a4()
            v1 = if a2.last ~= "Walk" then
                if a2.last ~= "Idle" then
                    if not (v2 < 0.4) then if not (v2 < 0.75) then "Peck" else "Walk" else "Idle"
                else
                    if not (v2 < 0.55) then "Walk" else "Peck"
            else
                if not (v2 < 0.7) then "Idle" else "Peck"
        elseif not (a2.walkCheckAt <= a3) then
            v1 = if not (a4() < 0.5) then "Graze" else "Idle"
        else
            everySeconds = u0.CowWalk.everySeconds
            a2.walkCheckAt = a3 + (everySeconds[1] + a4() * (everySeconds[2] - everySeconds[1]))
            v1 = if not (a4() < u0.CowWalk.chance) then "Graze" else "Walk"
        end
    end

    a2.last = v1

    if v1 == "Walk" then
        local walkStuds = Chicken.walkStuds
        v2 = walkStuds[1] + a4() * (walkStuds[2] - walkStuds[1])

        return v1, v2 / u0.WalkSpeed, v2
    end

    local v3 = Chicken.seconds[v1] or {1, 2}

    return v1, v3[1] + a4() * (v3[2] - v3[1]), 0
end

local function flowerText(a1) -- Line: 510; upvalues: u0 (val); types: a1: string
    local v1 = false

    for i, j in u0.FlowerSpecies do
        if string.sub(a1, -#j) == j then
            v1 = true
            break
        end
    end

    if not v1 then
        return nil
    end

    for k, n in u0.FlowerPatterns do
        if string.sub(a1, 1, #k) == k then
            return u0.FlowerText .. n
        end
    end

    return u0.FlowerText
end

for i, j in {"Red", "Yellow", "Blue", "White", "Orange", "Pink", "Purple", "Green", "Sky", "Lime", "Black"} do
    v1 = string.lower(j)
    Items_3 = u0.Items
    v2 = ("%*ClayBlock"):format(j)
    Items_3[v2] = (("A Clay Block colored %*. A Clay Block and any %* flower, at a Builder Bench or a Block Press."):format(v1, v1))
    Items_4 = u0.Items
    v2 = ("%*NeonBlock"):format(j)
    Items_4[v2] = (("Glows %*. A Glass Block and a Glowing %* flower, at a Builder Bench or a Block Press."):format(v1, v1))
    Items_5 = u0.Items
    v2 = ("%*GlassBlock"):format(j)
    Items_5[v2] = (("Glass colored %*. A Glass Block and any %* flower, at a Builder Bench or a Block Press."):format(v1, v1))
    Items_6 = u0.Items
    v2 = ("%*Tile"):format(j)
    Items_6[v2] = (("A thin %* tile to lay on a floor. Cut from a %* Clay Block at a Builder Bench."):format(v1, j))
end

    Items = u0.Items

end

function u0.of(a1) -- Line: 580; upvalues: u0 (val), flowerText (val); types: a1: string
    local v1 = u0.Items[a1] or u0.Nodes[a1] or flowerText(a1) or ""

    if u0.Soulbound[a1] and v1 ~= "" then
        return u0.SoulboundPrefix .. v1
    end

    return v1
end

function u3.mortenVariant(a1) -- Line: 792; upvalues: u3 (val); types: a1: string
    local v1 = string.lower(a1)

    for i, j in u3.Morten.variants do

function u3.mortenChance(a1) -- Line: 803; upvalues: u3 (val)
    if a1.odds then
        return 1 / a1.odds
    end

    local v1 = 0

    for i, j in u3.Morten.variants do
        if j.odds then
            v1 = v1 + 1 / j.odds
        end
    end

    return 1 - v1
end

function u3.rollMortenVariant(a1) -- Line: 817; upvalues: u3 (val); types: a1: number
    local variants = u3.Morten.variants
    local v1 = 0

    for i, j in variants do
        if j.odds then
            v1 = v1 + 1 / j.odds

            if a1 < v1 then
                return j
            end
        end
    end

    return variants[1]
end

function u3.encodeMortenPity(a1) -- Line: 854; upvalues: u3 (val); types: a1: table

    for i, j in u3.Morten.pity do
        table.insert(v1, (("%*=%*"):format(j.variants[1], a1[i] or 0)))
    end

    return table.concat(v1, ",")
end

function u3.decodeMortenPity(a1) -- Line: 862
    local v1 = {}

    if type(a1) ~= "string" then
        return v1
    end

    for i, j in string.gmatch(a1, "(%a+)=(%d+)") do
        v1[i] = (tonumber(j))
    end

    return v1
end

function u3.mortenGuaranteedIn(a1, a2) -- Line: 874; upvalues: u3 (val); types: a1: string, a2: number

    end

end

function u3.mortenGuaranteedWithin(a1, a2) -- Line: 887; upvalues: u3 (val); types: a1: string, a2: table
    local v1 = nil
    local v2 = {}

    for i, j in u3.Morten.pity do
        v2[i] = a2[j.variants[1]] or 0

        if j.variants[1] == a1 then
            v1 = i
        end
    end

    if not v1 then
        return nil
    end

    local v3 = u3.Morten.pity[v1].after + #u3.Morten.pity

    for k = 1, v3 do
        if u3.rollMortenWithPity(v2, 0.999999, 0).id == a1 then
            return k
        end
    end

    return v3
end

function u3.rollMortenWithPity(a1, a2, a3)
    -- Line: 911
    -- upvalues: u3 (val)
    -- types: a1: table, a2: number, a3: number
    local v1
    local v2 = nil

    for i, j in u3.Morten.pity do
        v1 = a1[i] or 0

        if j.after - 1 <= v1 then
            v2 = u3.mortenVariant(j.variants[math.clamp(math.floor(a3 * #j.variants) + 1, 1, #j.variants)])
            break
        end
    end

    v2 = v2 or u3.rollMortenVariant(a2)

    for k, n in u3.Morten.pity do
        if not table.find(n.variants, v2.id) then
            a1[k] = (a1[k] or 0) + 1
        else
            a1[k] = 0
        end
    end

    return v2
end

function u3.chooseFrom(a1, a2) -- Line: 987; types: a1: table, a2: number
    local v1 = 0

    for i, j in a1 do
        v1 = v1 + j.weight
    end

    local v2 = math.clamp(a2, 0, 0.999999) * v1
    v1 = 0

    for k, n in a1 do
        v1 = v1 + n.weight

        if v2 < v1 then
            return n.enemyId
        end
    end

    return a1[1].enemyId
end

u3.SharedLoot = {Players = 2, MinShareOfHP = 0.1}

function u3.sharedLootWinners(a1, a2) -- Line: 1136; upvalues: u3 (val); types: a1: table, a2: number
    local v1 = a2 * u3.SharedLoot.MinShareOfHP
    local v2 = {}

    for i, j in a1 do
        if 0 < j.damage and v1 <= j.damage then
            table.insert(v2, j)
        end
    end

    table.sort(
        v2,
        function(a1, a2) -- Line: 1144
            if a1.damage ~= a2.damage then
                return a2.damage < a1.damage
            end

            return a1.order < a2.order
        end
    )

    v1 = {}

    for k = 1, (math.min(#v2, u3.SharedLoot.Players)) do
        table.insert(v1, v2[k].who)
    end

    return v1
end

u3.Knockback = {StudsPerHit = 7, Seconds = 0.22, MaxStuds = 12}

function u3.knockbackVelocity(a1, a2) -- Line: 1183; upvalues: u3 (val); types: a1: string, a2: vector
    local v1 = u3.Enemies[a1]
    local mass = if not v1 then 1 else if not v1.mass then 1 else v1.mass
    v1 = Vector3.new(a2.X, 0, a2.Z)

    if not (0.001 <= v1.Magnitude) then
        return (Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
    end

    return v1.Unit
        * ((math.min(u3.Knockback.MaxStuds, u3.Knockback.StudsPerHit / (math.max(0.1, mass))))
            / u3.Knockback.Seconds)
end

function u3.wanderDirection(a1, a2, a3) -- Line: 1211; types: a1: vector, a2: vector, a3: number
    local Magnitude = a1.Magnitude

    if Magnitude < 0.001 then
        return a2
    end

    local v1 = Magnitude / math.max(1, a3)

    if v1 >= 1 then
        return a1
    end

    return a2 * (1 - v1) + a1.Unit * v1
end

return u3

function u0.conveyorVelocity(a1, a2) -- Line: 365; upvalues: u0 (val); types: a1: string?, a2: number

    local v1 = u0.conveyorKind(a1)

    if not v1 then
        return u0.headingFrom(a2) * SpeedStudsPerSecond
    end

    local v2 = u0.headingFrom(a2 + v1.turnDegrees)

    if v1.risePerCell == 0 then
        return v2 * SpeedStudsPerSecond
    end

    local v3 = if not (0 < v1.risePerCell) then -1 else 1

    return (v2 * v1.runCells + Vector3.new(0, v3, 0)).Unit * SpeedStudsPerSecond
        * u0.ConveyorRampSpeedMultiplier
end

function u0.conveyorIntakeDirection(a1, a2) -- Line: 402; upvalues: u0 (val); types: a1: string?, a2: number

        end

    end

    return nil
end

function u0.cornerRotationFacing(a1, a2) -- Line: 422; upvalues: u0 (val); types: a1: string?, a2: vector

function u0.conveyorLengthCells(a1) -- Line: 450; upvalues: u0 (val); types: a1: string?

    end

end

function u0.abilityAt(a1, a2) -- Line: 592; upvalues: u0 (val); types: a1: string, a2: number
    local v1 = u0.Abilities[a1]

    if not v1 then
        return nil
    end

    local v2 = table.clone(v1)
    local scales = v1.scales
    local v3 = u0.effectiveness(a2)

    if scales and type(v1[scales]) == "number" then
        if scales == "cooldownSeconds" then
            v2[scales] = v1[scales] / v3
            return v2
        end

        if scales == "chance" then
            v2[scales] = (math.min(1, v1[scales] * v3))
            return v2
        end

        v2[scales] = v1[scales] * v3
    end

    return v2
end

local function percentText(a1) -- Line: 630; types: a1: number
    local v1 = a1 * 100

    if not (v1 >= 10) and v1 ~= math.floor(v1) then
        return (("%*%%"):format((string.format("%.1f", v1))))
    end

    return (("%*%%"):format((math.floor(v1 + 0.5))))
end

local function secondsText(a1) -- Line: 637; types: a1: number
    if not (a1 >= 90) then
        if a1 == math.floor(a1) then
            return (("%* s"):format(a1))
        end

        return (("%* s"):format((string.format("%.1f", a1))))
    end

    local v1 = a1 / 60

    if v1 == math.floor(v1) then
        return (("%* min"):format(v1))
    end

    return (("%* min"):format((string.format("%.1f", v1))))
end

    end

function u0.chooseMineral(a1, a2, a3) -- Line: 184; types: a1: table, a2: table, a3: number
    if #a1.mix == 0 then
        return nil
    end

    local v1 = {}

    for i, j in a1.mix do
        if (a2[j.nodeType] or 0) < j.minActive then
            table.insert(v1, j)
        end
    end

    local mix_2 = if not (#v1 > 0) then a1.mix else v1
    v1 = 0

    for k, n in mix_2 do
        v1 = v1 + math.max(0, n.weight)
    end

    if v1 <= 0 then
        return mix_2[1]
    end

    local v2 = math.clamp(a3, 0, 0.999999) * v1
    v1 = 0

    for m, i5 in mix_2 do
        v1 = v1 + math.max(0, i5.weight)

        if v2 < v1 then
            return i5
        end
    end

    return mix_2[#mix_2]
end

        (a5 - v1.min) / (v1.max - v1.min)

    if not a6 or not (a6 >= 2) then
        v3 = v2.min + (v2.max - v2.min) * v5
    else
        v1 = math.clamp(math.floor(v5 * (a6 - 1) + 0.5), 0, a6 - 1)
        v3 = v2.min + (v2.max - v2.min) * v1 / (a6 - 1)
    end

    local v6 = u0.StatSteps[a3] or 0

    return (math.clamp(if not (v6 <= 0) then tonumber((string.format("%.6f", (math.round(v3 / v6)) * v6))) else v3, math.min(v2.min, v2.max), (math.max(v2.min, v2.max))))
end

function u0.fillMissing(a1, a2, a3, a4, a5)
    -- Line: 395
    -- upvalues: u0 (val)
    -- types: a1: table, a2: string, a3: table?, a4: function
    local v1
    local v2 = {}
    local v3 = false
    local v4 = nil
    local v5 = nil
    local v6, v7, v8, v9, v10 = a3, a2, a4, a5, a1

    for i, j in a1, v4, v5 do
        if not v6 or type(v6[i]) ~= "number" then
            v2[i] = j
            v3 = true
        end
    end

    if not v3 then
        return nil, false
    end

    v3 = if not v6 then {} else table.clone(v6)

    for k, n in (u0.rollStats(v2, v7, v8, v9)) do
        v3[k] = n
    end

    local v11 = true

    for m, i5 in v10 do
        v1 = u0.deriveBand(i5, m, v7)
        v1 = math.abs(v3[m] - (u0.bestValue(v1, m)))

        if v1 > 1e-09 then
            v11 = false
        end
    end

    return v3, v11
end

return u0

function u999.isSecret(a1) -- Line: 415; upvalues: u999 (val); types: a1: string?
    local v1 = false

    if a1 ~= nil then
        v1 = u999.Secret[a1] == true
    end

    return v1
end

    end

    if a3 == a2 then
        return false, "You're already on this island"
    end

    if table.find(a1.members, a3) ~= nil then
        return false, "They're already on this island"
    end

    if table.find(a1.invites, a3) ~= nil then
        return false, "They already have an invite"
    end

    if a5 <= #a1.invites then
        return false, "Too many invites out. Cancel one first."
    end

    if a4 <= u0.seatsTaken(a1) then
        return false, (("This island is full (%*)"):format(a4))
    end

    return true, nil
end

function u0.canAccept(a1, a2, a3) -- Line: 152; types: a1: table, a2: number, a3: number
    if table.find(a1.members, a2) ~= nil then
        return false, "You're already on this island"
    end

    if not (table.find(a1.invites, a2) ~= nil) then
        return false, "That invite is no longer open"
    end

    if a3 <= #a1.members then
        return false, "That island filled up"
    end

    return true, nil
end

function u0.canCancel(a1, a2, a3) -- Line: 165; upvalues: u0 (val); types: a1: table, a2: number, a3: number
    if not u0.isOwner(a1, a2) then
        return false, "Only the owner can cancel invites"
    end

    if not (table.find(a1.invites, a3) ~= nil) then
        return false, "There's no invite to cancel"
    end

    return true, nil
end

function u0.canRemove(a1, a2, a3) -- Line: 177; types: a1: table, a2: number, a3: number
    if a3 == a1.owner then
        return false, "The owner can't be removed"
    end

    if not (table.find(a1.members, a3) ~= nil) then
        return false, "They aren't on this island"
    end

    if a2 ~= a1.owner and a2 ~= a3 then
        return false, "Only the owner can remove someone"
    end

    return true, nil
end

function u0.withMember(a1, a2) -- Line: 197; types: a1: table, a2: number
    local v1 = table.clone(a1.members)

    if not (table.find(v1, a2) ~= nil) then
        table.insert(v1, a2)
    end

    local v2 = {}

    for i, j in a1.invites do
        if j ~= a2 then
            table.insert(v2, j)
        end
    end

    return {owner = a1.owner, name = a1.name, members = v1, invites = v2}
end

function u0.withoutMember(a1, a2) -- Line: 211; types: a1: table, a2: number
    local v1 = {}

    for i, j in a1.members do
        if j ~= a2 then
            table.insert(v1, j)
        end
    end

    return {
        owner = a1.owner,
        name = a1.name,
        members = v1,
        invites = table.clone(a1.invites),
    }
end

function u0.withInvite(a1, a2) -- Line: 221; types: a1: table, a2: number
    local v1 = table.clone(a1.invites)

    if not (table.find(v1, a2) ~= nil) and not (table.find(a1.members, a2) ~= nil) then
        table.insert(v1, a2)
    end

    return {
        owner = a1.owner,
        name = a1.name,
        members = table.clone(a1.members),
        invites = v1,
    }
end

function u0.isInvited(a1, a2) -- Line: 231; types: a1: table, a2: number
    return table.find(a1.invites, a2) ~= nil
end

function u0.withoutInvite(a1, a2) -- Line: 235; types: a1: table, a2: number
    local v1 = {}

    for i, j in a1.invites do
        if j ~= a2 then
            table.insert(v1, j)
        end
    end

    return {
        owner = a1.owner,
        name = a1.name,
        members = table.clone(a1.members),
        invites = v1,
    }
end

return u0

    local v1 = {}

    for i, j in u0.list do
        if not v1[j.group] then

    end

    return v2
end

function u0.signature(a1) -- Line: 1332; types: a1: table
    local v1 = {(("/%*"):format(a1.name))}
    local v2 = nil
    local v3 = nil

    for i, j in a1.args, v2, v3 do
        table.insert(v1, if not j.optional then ("<%*>"):format(j.name) else ("[%*]"):format(j.name))
    end

    return table.concat(v1, " ")
end

############################################################
SCRIPT NUMBER: 230
NAME: DisplayAccepts
CLASS: ModuleScript
PATH: ReplicatedStorage.Shared.DisplayAccepts
############################################################

EXTRACTION STATUS: SUCCESS
SOURCE LENGTH: 1107 characters

-- Script path: game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Shared.DisplayAccepts
-- Decompile time: 0.71 ms

local Config = require(script.Parent.Config)
return {
    takes = function(a1, a2) -- Line: 11; upvalues: Config (val); types: a1: string?, a2: string
        local v1

        if a1 == nil then
            return true
        end

        if a1 == "Weapon" then
            v1 = true

            if Config.Weapon.Weapons[a2] == nil then
                v1 = Config.Tool.Tools[a2] ~= nil
            end

            return v1
        end

        if a1 == "Armor" then
            v1 = Config.Wearable.slotOf(a2)
            local v2 = false

            if v1 ~= nil then
                v2 = Config.Wearable.isEnabled(v1)
            end

            return v2
        end

        if a1 == "Shiny" then
            return Config.Shiny.isShiny(a2)
        end

        if a1 == "Flower" then
            return Config.Flower.isFlower(a2)
        end

        if a1 == "Food" then
            return Config.Food.isFood(a2) and Config.Recipe.Food[a2] ~= nil
        end

        return false
    end,
}

function u0.coins(a1) -- Line: 29; upvalues: u0 (val); types: a1: number

function u0.short(a1, a2) -- Line: 55; upvalues: u0 (val); types: a1: number, a2: boolean?

function u0.count(a1) -- Line: 82; types: a1: number

    local v4 = math.floor(a1)

        return (tostring(v4))
    end

    end

        v1 = j[1]

            v2 = v4 / v1

local function dyedColorWord(a1) -- Line: 451; upvalues: u1288 (val), u1197 (val); types: a1: string
    local v1, v2

    if a1 == "WoolCarpet" then
        return "WoolBlock", "Carpet"
    end

    local v3 = string.match(a1, "^(%a+)WoolBlock$") or string.match(a1, "^(%a+)Carpet$")

    if v3 and u1288[v3] then
        v1 = v3

        if string.sub(a1, -6) == "Carpet" then
            return v1, "Carpet"
        end

        return v1, "Wool"
    end

    v1, v2 = string.match(a1, "^(%u%l+)(%u%l+)Block$")

    if v1 then
        if v2 ~= "Clay" and v2 ~= "Neon" and v2 ~= "Glass" and v2 ~= "Wool" then
            v2 = string.match(a1, "^(%u%l+)Tile$")

            if v2 and u1197[v2] then
                return v2, "Tile"
            end

            return nil, nil
        end

        if u1197[v1] then
            return v1, v2
        end
    end

    v2 = string.match(a1, "^(%u%l+)Tile$")

    if v2 and u1197[v2] then
        return v2, "Tile"
    end

    return nil, nil
end

    end

        return u1197[FlowerConfig.colorOf(a1) or ""] or u1196

function u23.materialFor(a1) -- Line: 665; upvalues: u1388 (val), dyedColorWord (val); types: a1: string
    local v1

    _, v1 = dyedColorWord(a1)

        if v1 == "Glass" then

        end

    end

    return Enum.Material.Plastic
end

    }
end

function u6.step(a1, a2, a3, a4) -- Line: 53; types: a1: table, a2: number, a3: userdata, a4: userdata?

        v1.FilterType = a4.FilterType
        v1.FilterDescendantsInstances = a4.FilterDescendantsInstances

        if PartBoundsInRadius[1] then
            return {
                kind = "target",
                position = position,
                part = PartBoundsInRadius[1],
                normal = -v2.Unit,

        end
    end

    local Distance = if not v4 then Magnitude else v4.Distance

            a1.position = v6

            position = v4.Position,
            part = v4.Instance,

    a1.travelled = a1.travelled + Magnitude
